P209

Summary

kb/reports/P209.summary.txt

P209 (0 calls). STOPPED before step 1's write (user decision in this unit): kb/compiled is NOT rebuilt; it stays the P201 A compile. The rebuild was done in sandboxes only (kb/tmp/P209, git-ignored) and is reported here. Nothing else was written: no binding reference, no audits, no gates.csv, no cards; kb/<pid>.figures.json and form_versions.csv untouched; psed_v2/agent/ untouched. Prompts: 409 files (every pass-0 prompt, pass-1 prefix and group, 0ax prompt of the thirteen, with schemas) built before: identical to P207b's; kb/compiled was not written, so no prompt can have changed.
Why stopped: the rebuild applies P201 B's rule (compile.figure_quantity: a figure label takes the latest 0ax answer; a label with no answer is other). Ten of the thirteen papers have no 0ax line (only ela03, gre23, yim22 do), so every legend key and part of theirs resolves to other: 944 fact rows change (hei24 409, zou23 246, wer21 154, yim20 43, art19 41, ...): hei24's exposure, molar_mass, saturation_density, tortuosity_over_porosity, surface_to_pore_volume_ratio settings become other; art19's pulse times, wer21's plasma powers and pulse times, yim20's feature widths and cycle numbers likewise; zou23's legend temperatures become other, so its units are no longer matched by conditions (derived units appear). Matched gold falls 2576 -> 2473: hei24 391 -> 304 (setting 95 -> 22, comparison 15 -> 1), yim20 524 -> 514, art19 99 -> 90, gao15 117 -> 116; zou23 71 -> 75. Every one of these is P201 B's; the P204-P207b rules together net 0 (yim22 -1, gre23 +1). Asked (user): stop and report only; next step yours (e.g. 0ax for the ten papers, which costs calls, or a rule for a paper with no 0ax line).
Method: one sandbox per compile-code state, all on today's data (records, form_versions.csv, history.csv; figures.json unchanged since P201 A): s00 P201 A (3c5204a0), s01 P201 B, s02 P202, s03-s06 P204 A1 / A2 / A3 / A4 (P204 A's hunks applied one rule at a time; the four together equal 6d3da000), s07 P204 B-C, s08 P205, s09 P206-P206b, s10-s12 P207 rules 1 (with P207b), 2, 3 (s12 = HEAD 2fedf44e) (P209.0_stages.csv). s00 reproduces kb/compiled's facts, experiments and binding byte for byte (P209.0_s00_reproduces_kb_compiled.csv: compile.log and summary.csv differ only in the log header and the new records lines listed as ignored). A fact's change is charged to the stage that made it (P209.1_facts_changed.csv; per paper and rule P209.1_counts.csv).
Facts changed by rule (fact rows): P201 B 944; P202 0; P204 A1 21 (yim22 15, yim20 4, yli18 2; species.csv 179 rows, P209.2_A1_species.csv); P204 A2 0 (no transform row changed); P204 A3 109 (gor03 17, mat22 16, ela03 13, yim22 22, wer21 14, yli18 10, yim20 8, gao15 6, gre23 3; the merges P209.2_A3_merges.csv); P204 A4 2 (ela03: one 50% penetration depth at its feature's end, P209.2_A4_front_at_end.csv); P204 B-C 0; P205 2 (gre23; the parts and their outcomes P209.2_P205_distinguished.csv); P206-P206b 0; P207 rule 1 5 (the 9 records dropped with their quotes, P209.2_rule1_quote_value.csv: yim20 5, yli18 2, hei24 1, wer21 1); P207 rule 2 11 (ela03 10, zou23 1; P209.2_rule2_species.csv); P207 rule 3 0 (6 transforms rows whose listed inputs change, P209.2_rule3_transforms.csv).
Gold per type (P209.3_gold_matched.csv: P201 A, after P201 B, P209) and every gold row lost or gained with the stage that did it, the fact it matched before and what its unit holds now (P209.3_gold_changes.csv): 107 lost and 4 gained at P201 B; yim22 1 gained at P204 A1 (tabu1.r21c0#1), 2 lost at P204 A3 (s14.p6#6, s14.p6#9: merged into fuller facts); gre23 1 gained at P205 (s9.p9#3).
Binding (P209.4_binding_moved.csv): 53 items move, all at P201 B (zou23 29, wer21 24). binding.py --check fails today on HEAD code with kb/compiled untouched, because it rebinds with the current code: 48 items lost their unit (zou23 24, wer21 24), all P201 B moves (P209.4_binding_check_HEAD.csv); it has failed since P201 B (no unit ran it since).
Checks run (read-only): export --check: vocab.py and schema/ up to date, 21 checks pass; gold readability: 12 of 12 papers, 5357 rows read by compile.gold_rows (P209.5_gold_readability.csv).
